  2527	     1	// In-memory fs fake for unit tests. Synchronous subset only.
  2528	     2	// Atomicity contract: renameSync moves content in a single step, so a target
  2529	     3	// path is never observed carrying partial content. Every mutation appends a
  2530	     4	// full snapshot to `fs.__history`, letting tests enumerate intermediate states
  2531	     5	// and prove the tmp+rename discipline (target's first appearance is a rename).
  2532	     6	
  2533	     7	function fsError(code, syscall, path) {
  2534	     8	  const e = new Error(`${code}: ${syscall} '${path}'`);
  2535	     9	  e.code = code;
  2536	    10	  e.syscall = syscall;
  2537	    11	  e.path = path;
  2538	    12	  return e;
  2539	    13	}
  2540	    14	
  2541	    15	function normalize(p) {
  2542	    16	  const str = String(p);
  2543	    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
  2544	    18	  const out = [];
  2545	    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
  2546	    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
  2547	    21	    if (part === '..') {
  2548	    22	      out.pop();
  2549	    23	      continue;
  2550	    24	    }
  2551	    25	    out.push(part);
  2552	    26	  }
  2553	    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
  2554	    28	}
  2555	    29	
  2556	    30	function dirname(np) {
  2557	    31	  const i = np.lastIndexOf('/');
  2558	    32	  if (i <= 0) return '/';
  2559	    33	  return np.slice(0, i);
  2560	    34	}
  2561	    35	
  2562	    36	function basename(np, parent) {
  2563	    37	  return parent === '/' ? np.slice(1) : np.slice(parent.length + 1);
  2564	    38	}
  2565	    39	
  2566	    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
  2567	    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
  2568	    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
  2569	    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
  2570	    44	  const history = [];
  2571	    45	  let clock = 0;
  2572	    46	
  2573	    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
  2574	    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
  2575	    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
  2576	    50	
  2577	    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
  2578	    52	    let cur = '';
  2579	    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
  2580	    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
  2581	    55	      dirs.add(cur);
  2582	    56	    }
  2583	    57	  };
  2584	    58	
  2585	    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
  2586	    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
  2587	    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
  2588	    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
  2589	    63	  };
  2590	    64	
  2591	    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
  2592	    66	    const np = normalize(p);
  2593	    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
  2594	    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
  2595	    69	    touch(np);
  2596	    70	  }
  2597	    71	
  2598	    72	  const fs = {
  2599	    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
  2600	    74	      const np = normalize(p);
  2601	    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
  2602	    76	      return store.get(np);
  2603	    77	    },
  2604	    78	
  2605	    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
  2606	    80	      const np = normalize(p);
  2607	    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  2608	    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
  2609	    83	      touch(np);
  2610	    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
  2611	    85	    },
  2612	    86	
  2613	    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
  2614	    88	      const np = normalize(p);
  2615	    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  2616	    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
  2617	    91	      touch(np);
  2618	    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
  2619	    93	    },
  2620	    94	
  2621	    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
  2622	    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
  2623	    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
  2624	    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
  2625	    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  2626	   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
  2627	   101	        store.delete(nf);
  2628	   102	        store.set(nt, content);
  2629	   103	        touch(nt);
  2630	   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  2631	   105	        return;
  2632	   106	      }
  2633	   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
  2634	   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
  2635	   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
  2636	   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  2637	   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
  2638	   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
  2639	   113	        dirs.add(nt);
  2640	   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
  2641	   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
  2642	   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
  2643	   117	            store.delete(k);
  2644	   118	          }
  2645	   119	        }
  2646	   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
  2647	   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
  2648	   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
  2649	   123	            dirs.delete(d);
  2650	   124	          }
  2651	   125	        }
  2652	   126	        touch(nt);
  2653	   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  2654	   128	        return;
  2655	   129	      }
  2656	   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
  2657	   131	    },
  2658	   132	
  2659	   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
  2660	   134	      const np = normalize(p);
  2661	   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
  2662	   136	        mkdirp(np);
  2663	   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  2664	   138	        return undefined;
  2665	   139	      }
  2666	   140	      ensureParent(np, 'mkdir');
  2667	   141	      if (dirs.has(np) || store.has(np)) throw fsError('EEXIST', 'mkdir', np);
  2668	   142	      dirs.add(np);
  2669	   143	      record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  2670	   144	      return undefined;
  2671	   145	    },
  2672	   146	
  2673	   147	    existsSync(p) {
  2674	   148	      const np = normalize(p);
  2675	   149	      return store.has(np) || dirs.has(np);
  2676	   150	    },
  2677	   151	
  2678	   152	    readdirSync(p) {
  2679	   153	      const np = normalize(p);
  2680	   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {
  2681	   155	        if (store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOTDIR', 'scandir', np);
  2682	   156	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'scandir', np);
  2683	   157	      }
  2684	   158	      const names = new Set();
  2685	   159	      const collect = (k) => {
  2686	   160	        if (k !== np && dirname(k) === np) names.add(basename(k, np));
  2687	   161	      };
  2688	   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
  2689	   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
  2690	   164	      return [...names];
  2691	   165	    },
  2692	   166	
  2693	   167	    unlinkSync(p) {
  2694	   168	      const np = normalize(p);
  2695	   169	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'unlink', np);
  2696	   170	      store.delete(np);
  2697	   171	      record('unlinkSync', [np]);
  2698	   172	    },
  2699	   173	
  2700	   174	    rmSync(p, opts = {}) {
  2701	   175	      const np = normalize(p);
  2702	   176	      const isFile = store.has(np);
  2703	   177	      const isDir = dirs.has(np);
  2704	   178	      if (!isFile && !isDir) {
  2705	   179	        if (opts.force) return;
  2706	   180	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rm', np);
  2707	   181	      }
  2708	   182	      if (isFile) store.delete(np);
  2709	   183	      if (isDir) {
  2710	   184	        const pre = np + '/';
  2711	   185	        if (!opts.recursive) {
  2712	   186	          const hasChildren = [...store.keys(), ...dirs].some((k) => k !== np && k.startsWith(pre));
  2713	   187	          if (hasChildren) throw fsError('ENOTEMPTY', 'rmdir', np);
  2714	   188	          dirs.delete(np);
  2715	   189	        } else {
  2716	   190	          for (const k of [...store.keys()]) if (k === np || k.startsWith(pre)) store.delete(k);
  2717	   191	          for (const d of [...dirs]) if (d === np || d.startsWith(pre)) dirs.delete(d);
  2718	   192	        }
  2719	   193	      }
  2720	   194	      record('rmSync', [np]);
  2721	   195	    },
  2722	   196	
  2723	   197	    statSync(p) {
  2724	   198	      const np = normalize(p);
  2725	   199	      if (store.has(np)) {
  2726	   200	        const size = Buffer.byteLength(store.get(np));
  2727	   201	        return { isFile: () => true, isDirectory: () => false, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  2728	   202	      }
  2729	   203	      if (dirs.has(np)) {
  2730	   204	        return { isFile: () => false, isDirectory: () => true, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size: 0, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  2731	   205	      }
  2732	   206	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'stat', np);
  2733	   207	    },
  2734	   208	
  2735	   209	    // The fake cannot represent symlinks, so lstat === stat and realpath is
  2736	   210	    // normalization. Symlink-refusal behavior is proven over the real fs in
  2737	   211	    // tests/integration/symlink-escape.test.mjs.
  2738	   212	    lstatSync(p) {
  2739	   213	      return fs.statSync(p);
  2740	   214	    },
  2741	   215	
  2742	   216	    realpathSync(p) {
  2743	   217	      const np = normalize(p);
  2744	   218	      if (!store.has(np) && !dirs.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'realpath', np);
  2745	   219	      return np;
  2746	   220	    },
  2747	   221	
  2748	   222	    copyFileSync(src, dest) {
  2749	   223	      const ns = normalize(src);
  2750	   224	      const nd = normalize(dest);
  2751	   225	      if (!store.has(ns)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'copyfile', ns);
  2752	   226	      ensureParent(nd, 'copyfile');
  2753	   227	      store.set(nd, store.get(ns));
  2754	   228	      touch(nd);
  2755	   229	      record('copyFileSync', [ns, nd]);
  2756	   230	    },
  2757	   231	  };
  2758	   232	
  2759	   233	  // Introspection hook for atomicity assertions; not part of the real fs API.
  2760	   234	  fs.__history = history;
  2761	   235	
  2762	   236	  return { fs, files: () => snapshot() };
  2763	   237	}

  2768	     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
  2769	     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
  2770	     3	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
  2771	     4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
  2772	     5	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
  2773	     6	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
  2774	     7	import { prepare, commit } from '../../core/src/receive/txn.mjs';
  2775	     8	import { loadBundle, bundlePaths, appendJournal } from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
  2776	     9	
  2777	    10	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2778	    11	// Contract choices for core/src/receive/txn.mjs (docs/design/core.md §Module APIs
  2779	    12	// prepare/commit; §Per-module test lists "txn"; plan §Concurrency — the receive
  2780	    13	// prepare/commit two-phase transaction, receipt-token bindings incl. the
  2781	    14	// content-sensitive git digest, open->received degraded seal, ownership adoption +
  2782	    15	// new generation, chained failover, competing receivers, double-commit).
  2783	    16	//
  2784	    17	// SIGNATURES (pinned — the task overrides core.md's 3-arg commit):
  2785	    18	//   prepare(root, opts, io) -> { token, prompt, assignments, warnings }
  2786	    19	//   commit(root, token, opts, io) -> { adopted, generation, archivedTo }
  2787	    20	//   Both are SYNCHRONOUS. The plan pins that the receive COMMAND wires
  2788	    21	//   core/src/git/snapshot.mjs in and passes the result as opts.gitSnapshot; txn
  2789	    22	//   itself never spawns git — it takes the snapshot as plain data.
  2790	    23	//
  2791	    24	//   opts (same shape for prepare and commit):
  2792	    25	//     { platform,       // destination harness we are resuming ON (e.g. 'codex')
  2793	    26	//       origin,         // intake: the harness handed off FROM (e.g. 'claude-code')
  2794	    27	//       reason,         // intake: why the switch happened (free text)
  2795	    28	//       gitSnapshot,    // the caller-computed git snapshot object (git/snapshot.mjs)
  2796	    29	//       probes,         // optional probe map for resolveRoles (default null)
  2797	    30	//       sessionHint }   // the RECEIVING session's hint, used for ownership adoption
  2798	    31	//
  2799	    32	// TOKEN BINDING (THE pin). prepare captures a REVISION FINGERPRINT and returns it
  2800	    33	//   as an opaque token. It binds every one of these inputs (one rejection test per
  2801	    34	//   input below — verifier fold F3 completed the set):
  2802	    35	//     - bundle REVISION: generation + journalSeq (as loadBundle reports them)
  2803	    36	//     - intake: origin + reason
  2804	    37	//     - config digest: a hash of the baton.config.json bytes at <root>
  2805	    38	//     - GIT digest: a hash of the WHOLE opts.gitSnapshot object (PIN — the git
  2806	    39	//       state is bound by hashing the caller-passed object, so headSha, dirty,
  2807	    40	//       AND contentDigest are each binding: a HEAD move with an UNCHANGED
  2808	    41	//       contentDigest still drifts the token, and vice versa)
  2809	    42	//     - probe snapshot: a hash of opts.probes
  2810	    43	//     - target platform (opts.platform)
  2811	    44	//     - receiving session (opts.sessionHint) — plan: the token binds "target
  2812	    45	//       platform+session"
  2813	    46	//   prepare mutates NOTHING (it cannot persist a "prepared" record — the token is
  2814	    47	//   self-contained). commit RE-DERIVES every bound input from current disk state +
  2815	    48	//   its own opts and compares to the token; ANY drift throws an Error whose message
  2816	    49	//   names re-prepare (matches /re-?prepare/i) and performs NO mutation (the
  2817	    50	//   .handoff tree is byte-identical before and after the rejected commit). Because
  2818	    51	//   a successful commit bumps `generation`, an already-spent token (double-commit)
  2819	    52	//   and a losing competitor's token (competing receivers) both fail the revision
  2820	    53	//   check with no extra bookkeeping.
  2821	    54	//
  2822	    55	// COMMIT SUCCESS (one atomic transition):
  2823	    56	//   - Archives the current bundle to history/ via rotateJournal kind 'receive'
  2824	    57	//     (a history/<stem>.receive.json + .ndjson pair). archivedTo === that stem.
  2825	    58	//     The archived .receive freeze is the RECEIVED seal (handoff.status
  2826	    59	//     'received' — asserted on BOTH the sealed and open paths, verifier fold F7:
  2827	    60	//     archiving the pre-commit sealed bundle unchanged would be wrong); it
  2828	    61	//     retains the receipt data (the receive_log entry for THIS receive rides in
  2829	    62	//     the archived freeze). For an OPEN source bundle it is the receive-generated
  2830	    63	//     DEGRADED seal (handoff.reason from intake, reasonClass classified from the
  2831	    64	//     reason text against the intake ORIGIN platform via the built-in signature
  2832	    65	//     table); for a SEALED source the finalize reason/reasonClass are retained.
  2833	    66	//   - Adopts ownership: origin becomes the RECEIVING side — origin.platform ===
  2834	    67	//     opts.platform, origin.sessionHint === opts.sessionHint.
  2835	    68	//   - Opens a fresh writable generation: generation + 1, handoff.status 'open'.
  2836	    69	//   - Appends a receive_log entry { origin, reason, at }; for the OPEN (degraded)
  2837	    70	//     path the entry additionally carries degradedSeal: true (PIN: that flag lives
  2838	    71	//     on the receive_log entry). The sealed path's entry has NO truthy degradedSeal.
  2839	    72	//   - Returns { adopted: <truthy>, generation: <new gen>, archivedTo: <stem> }.
  2840	    73	//
  2841	    74	// STALENESS (prepare warnings, plan §Receive): OPEN source bundle -> a warning
  2842	    75	//   notes the missing seal; age > 12h (now - finalizedAt), HEAD moved
  2843	    76	//   (bundle.git.headSha !== opts.gitSnapshot.headSha), and a dirty mismatch each
  2844	    77	//   add a warning. Asserted as substring matches, not byte-exact strings.
  2845	    78	//
  2846	    79	// All txn tests seed: the bundle, baton.config.json at <root>, AND the built-in
  2847	    80	// signature table at its module-resolved path (loadSignatures reads via io.fs), so
  2848	    81	// role resolution and reasonClass classification both work over memfs.
  2849	    82	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2850	    83	
  2851	    84	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  2852	    85	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
  2853	    86	// The exact path txn resolves for the built-in table (core/src/receive ->
  2854	    87	// ../../data), so seeding it here makes loadSignatures(io) succeed over memfs.
  2855	    88	const SIG_PATH = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
  2856	    89	const SIG_CONTENT = readFileSync(SIG_PATH, 'utf8');
  2857	    90	
  2858	    91	const ROOT = '/repo';
  2859	    92	const NOW = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
  2860	    93	
  2861	    94	const CONFIG = {
  2862	    95	  schema: 'baton/config@1',
  2863	    96	  roles: {
  2864	    97	    planner: ['claude-code/claude-fable-5'],
  2865	    98	    'plan-reviewer': ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh', 'claude-code/claude-fable-5@xhigh'],
  2866	    99	    'test-author': ['claude-code/claude-opus-4-8', 'codex/gpt-5.5@xhigh'],
  2867	   100	    'test-verifier': ['codex/gpt-5.5@xhigh', 'claude-code/claude-opus-4-8'],
  2868	   101	    implementer: ['claude-code/claude-fable-5', 'codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh', 'cursor/composer'],
  2869	   102	    'final-reviewer-a': ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh', 'codex/gpt-5.5@xhigh'],
  2870	   103	    'final-reviewer-b': ['claude-code/claude-fable-5@xhigh', 'claude-code/claude-opus-4-8'],
  2871	   104	  },
  2872	   105	  platforms: { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} },
  2873	   106	  defaults: { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' },
  2874	   107	};
  2875	   108	const CONFIG_TEXT = JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2);
  2876	   109	
  2877	   110	// A git snapshot as core/src/git/snapshot.mjs would return; headSha matches the
  2878	   111	// bundle's captured git so the non-stale tests raise no HEAD-moved warning.
  2879	   112	const G1 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-1' };
  2880	   113	const G2 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-2-CHANGED' };
  2881	   114	
  2882	   115	const snapText = (b) => JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n';
  2883	   116	
  2884	   117	function baseBundle(overrides = {}) {
  2885	   118	  return {
  2886	   119	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
  2887	   120	    bundleId: 'b_txn0000000000',
  2888	   121	    generation: 1,
  2889	   122	    createdAt: NOW,
  2890	   123	    updatedAt: NOW,
  2891	   124	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-A', unstable: false },
  2892	   125	    task: { goal: 'Wire the receive transaction', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
  2893	   126	    plan: { steps: [{ id: 's1', title: 'Bind the token', status: 'active', note: null }] },
  2894	   127	    decisions: [],
  2895	   128	    files: { touched: [] },
  2896	   129	    roles: { assignments: {} },
  2897	   130	    git: { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'd0', summaryTruncated: false },
  2898	   131	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
  2899	   132	    journalSeq: 5,
  2900	   133	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
  2901	   134	    dedupeRing: [],
  2902	   135	    ...overrides,
  2903	   136	  };
  2904	   137	}
  2905	   138	
  2906	   139	function sealedBundle(overrides = {}) {
  2907	   140	  return baseBundle({
  2908	   141	    handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: "You've hit your usage limit", reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: 'codex', finalizedAt: NOW, receive_log: [] },
  2909	   142	    ...overrides,
  2910	   143	  });
  2911	   144	}
  2912	   145	
  2913	   146	function openBundle(overrides = {}) {
  2914	   147	  return baseBundle({ handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] }, ...overrides });
  2915	   148	}
  2916	   149	
  2917	   150	function makeTxnIo({ bundle, journal, configText = CONFIG_TEXT, now = NOW, extra = {} } = {}) {
  2918	   151	  const files = {
  2919	   152	    [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT,
  2920	   153	    [`${ROOT}/baton.config.json`]: configText,
  2921	   154	    [`${ROOT}/.handoff/bundle.json`]: snapText(bundle),
  2922	   155	    ...extra,
  2923	   156	  };
  2924	   157	  if (journal !== undefined) files[`${ROOT}/.handoff/journal.ndjson`] = journal;
  2925	   158	  return makeIo({ files, now });
  2926	   159	}
  2927	   160	
  2928	   161	// Standard opts for a claude-code -> codex receive.
  2929	   162	function opts(overrides = {}) {
  2930	   163	  return { platform: 'codex', origin: 'claude-code', reason: 'switching to codex', gitSnapshot: G1, probes: null, sessionHint: 'codex-sess-1', ...overrides };
  2931	   164	}
  2932	   165	
  2933	   166	const readHistoryFreeze = (io, stem) => JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${bundlePaths(ROOT).historyDir}/${stem}.json`, 'utf8'));
  2934	   167	
  2935	   168	// ===========================================================================
  2936	   169	describe('receive-txn.prepare — sealed bundle', () => {
  2937	   170	  it('returns {token, prompt, assignments, warnings} and mutates NOTHING', () => {
  2938	   171	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle() });
  2939	   172	    const before = io.files();
  2940	   173	
  2941	   174	    const out = prepare(ROOT, opts(), io);
  2942	   175	
  2943	   176	    assert.ok(typeof out.token === 'string' && out.token.length > 0, 'token is an opaque non-empty string');
  2944	   177	    assert.ok(typeof out.prompt === 'string', 'prompt is a string (from receive/prompt.mjs)');
  2945	   178	    assert.ok(out.prompt.includes('Wire the receive transaction'), 'prompt carries the goal (delegation to prompt.mjs)');
  2946	   179	    assert.ok(out.prompt.includes('unverified claims to check against the working tree'), 'prompt carries the claims posture');
  2947	   180	    assert.ok(out.assignments && typeof out.assignments === 'object', 'assignments is an object');
  2948	   181	    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(out.assignments), Object.keys(CONFIG.roles), 'one assignment per configured role');
  2949	   182	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(out.warnings), 'warnings is an array');
  2950	   183	
  2951	   184	    assert.deepEqual(io.files(), before, 'prepare is read-only: no file may change');
  2952	   185	  });
  2953	   186	
  2954	   187	  it('the token is opaque and different from a token bound to a different intake', () => {
  2955	   188	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle() });
  2956	   189	    const t1 = prepare(ROOT, opts({ reason: 'reason one' }), io).token;
  2957	   190	    const t2 = prepare(ROOT, opts({ reason: 'reason two' }), io).token;
  2958	   191	    assert.notEqual(t1, t2, 'binding a different reason yields a different token');
  2959	   192	  });
  2960	   193	});
  2961	   194	
  2962	   195	// ===========================================================================
  2963	   196	describe('receive-txn.prepare — open bundle (limit death, no seal)', () => {
  2964	   197	  it('works and warns about the missing seal', () => {
  2965	   198	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle() });
  2966	   199	    const out = prepare(ROOT, opts(), io);
  2967	   200	    assert.ok(typeof out.token === 'string' && out.token.length > 0);
  2968	   201	    assert.ok(
  2969	   202	      out.warnings.some((w) => typeof w === 'string' && /seal|unseal|open|degrad/i.test(w)),
  2970	   203	      'an open (unsealed) source bundle must surface a missing-seal warning',
  2971	   204	    );
  2972	   205	  });
  2973	   206	
  2974	   207	  it('surfaces staleness warnings: age > 12h, HEAD moved, and a dirty mismatch', () => {
  2975	   208	    const staleBundle = sealedBundle({
  2976	   209	      handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: 'r', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: 'codex', finalizedAt: '2026-07-10T04:00:00.000Z', receive_log: [] },
  2977	   210	      git: { branch: 'main', headSha: 'OLDsha', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'd0', summaryTruncated: false },
  2978	   211	    });
  2979	   212	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: staleBundle, now: NOW });
  2980	   213	    // gitSnapshot reports a MOVED head and a dirty tree — both diverge from capture.
  2981	   214	    const movedDirty = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'NEWsha', dirty: true, dirtySummary: [' M x.mjs'], contentDigest: 'gd-x' };
  2982	   215	    const out = prepare(ROOT, opts({ gitSnapshot: movedDirty }), io);
  2983	   216	    const joined = out.warnings.join('\n');
  2984	   217	    assert.match(joined, /12|stale|h ago|hour/i, 'age > 12h must warn');
  2985	   218	    assert.match(joined, /HEAD|head/, 'a moved HEAD must warn');
  2986	   219	    assert.match(joined, /dirt/i, 'a dirty mismatch must warn');
  2987	   220	  });
  2988	   221	});
  2989	   222	
  2990	   223	// ===========================================================================
  2991	   224	describe('receive-txn.commit — sealed -> received (normal), ownership adoption + new generation', () => {
  2992	   225	  it('archives via rotateJournal "receive", adopts the receiver as owner, opens generation+1, appends receive_log', () => {
  2993	   226	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  2994	   227	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, opts(), io);
  2995	   228	
  2996	   229	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, opts(), io);
  2997	   230	
  2998	   231	    assert.ok(res.adopted, 'commit reports adoption');
  2999	   232	    assert.equal(res.generation, 2, 'a fresh writable generation is opened (1 -> 2)');
  3000	   233	    assert.ok(typeof res.archivedTo === 'string' && res.archivedTo.endsWith('.receive'), 'archivedTo is the receive rotation stem');
  3001	   234	
  3002	   235	    // A "receive" rotation landed in history.
  3003	   236	    const names = io.fs.readdirSync(bundlePaths(ROOT).historyDir);
  3004	   237	    assert.ok(names.some((n) => /\.receive\.json$/.test(n)), 'a receive freeze exists in history/');
  3005	   238	
  3006	   239	    // The live bundle: adopted owner, open, gen 2, receive_log appended.
  3007	   240	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  3008	   241	    assert.equal(bundle.generation, 2);
  3009	   242	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'the new live generation is writable (open)');
  3010	   243	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.platform, 'codex', 'ownership adopted to the receiving platform');
  3011	   244	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.sessionHint, 'codex-sess-1', 'ownership adopted to the receiving session');
  3012	   245	    const entry = bundle.handoff.receive_log.at(-1);
  3013	   246	    assert.ok(entry, 'a receive_log entry was appended');
  3014	   247	    assert.equal(entry.origin, 'claude-code', 'receive_log records the intake origin');
  3015	   248	    assert.equal(entry.reason, 'switching to codex', 'receive_log records the intake reason');
  3016	   249	    assert.ok(typeof entry.at === 'string' && entry.at.length > 0, 'receive_log records a timestamp');
  3017	   250	    assert.ok(!entry.degradedSeal, 'a sealed->received commit is NOT a degraded seal');
  3018	   251	  });
  3019	   252	
  3020	   253	  it('(F7) the archived freeze is the RECEIVED seal with receipt data, not the pre-commit sealed bundle', () => {
  3021	   254	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3022	   255	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, opts(), io);
  3023	   256	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, opts(), io);
  3024	   257	
  3025	   258	    const seal = readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo);
  3026	   259	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.status, 'received', 'the archived freeze records the sealed->received transition');
  3027	   260	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reason, "You've hit your usage limit", 'the finalize reason is retained on the archived seal');
  3028	   261	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'the finalize reasonClass is retained');
  3029	   262	
  3030	   263	    const archived = seal.handoff.receive_log.at(-1);
  3031	   264	    assert.ok(archived, 'the archived seal retains the receive_log receipt data for this receive');
  3032	   265	    assert.equal(archived.origin, 'claude-code');
  3033	   266	    assert.equal(archived.reason, 'switching to codex');
  3034	   267	    assert.ok(typeof archived.at === 'string' && archived.at.length > 0);
  3035	   268	  });
  3036	   269	});
  3037	   270	
  3038	   271	// ===========================================================================
  3039	   272	describe('receive-txn.commit — open -> received (degraded seal)', () => {
  3040	   273	  it('writes a degraded seal: receive_log entry {degradedSeal:true}, reasonClass classified from the reason', () => {
  3041	   274	    // A usage-limit reason string for the claude-code origin -> classifies usage-limit.
  3042	   275	    const reason = "You've hit your session limit · resets 3pm";
  3043	   276	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });
  3044	   277	    const o = opts({ reason });
  3045	   278	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  3046	   279	
  3047	   280	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  3048	   281	    assert.equal(res.generation, 2);
  3049	   282	
  3050	   283	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  3051	   284	    const entry = bundle.handoff.receive_log.at(-1);
  3052	   285	    assert.equal(entry.degradedSeal, true, 'PIN: the degraded flag lives on the receive_log entry');
  3053	   286	    assert.equal(entry.origin, 'claude-code');
  3054	   287	    assert.equal(entry.reason, reason);
  3055	   288	
  3056	   289	    // The archived "receive" freeze is the degraded seal: status received, reason
  3057	   290	    // from intake, reasonClass classified from the reason text (origin platform).
  3058	   291	    const seal = readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo);
  3059	   292	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.status, 'received', 'the archived freeze is the received seal');
  3060	   293	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reason, reason, 'the degraded seal records the intake reason');
  3061	   294	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'reasonClass is classified from the reason text');
  3062	   295	  });
  3063	   296	
  3064	   297	  it('(F10) a LOW-confidence classification is NOT sealed — reasonClass stays null (no laundering)', () => {
  3065	   298	    // A cursor "quota exceeded" reason matches usage-limit only at LOW
  3066	   299	    // confidence. Sealing it would make the NEXT prepare trust it as high
  3067	   300	    // confidence and auto-avoid the origin. The degraded seal must leave
  3068	   301	    // reasonClass null so the next receive re-classifies at its true confidence.
  3069	   302	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });
  3070	   303	    const o = opts({ origin: 'cursor', reason: 'quota exceeded' });
  3071	   304	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  3072	   305	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  3073	   306	
  3074	   307	    const seal = readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo);
  3075	   308	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'a low-confidence class is not laundered into the seal');
  3076	   309	
  3077	   310	    // And the fresh open generation likewise carries no trusted class.
  3078	   311	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  3079	   312	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'the new open generation inherits no low-confidence class');
  3080	   313	  });
  3081	   314	
  3082	   315	  it('(F10) an EXPLICIT --reason-class still seals (confirmed intake is high-confidence)', () => {
  3083	   316	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });
  3084	   317	    const o = opts({ origin: 'cursor', reason: 'quota exceeded', reasonClass: 'usage-limit' });
  3085	   318	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  3086	   319	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  3087	   320	    assert.equal(readHistoryFreeze(io, res.archivedTo).handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'explicit intake seals');
  3088	   321	  });
  3089	   322	
  3090	   323	  it('(iter-4 I3) the fresh open generation carries NO prior reason/class/finalizedAt/toPlatformHint', () => {
  3091	   324	    // Even a HIGH-confidence sealed usage-limit receive must not leave its class
  3092	   325	    // on the new writable generation: the next prepare treats a sealed
  3093	   326	    // reasonClass as high-confidence and would auto-avoid the freshly adopted
  3094	   327	    // origin. Only receive_log (the audit chain) carries forward.
  3095	   328	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3096	   329	    const o = opts();
  3097	   330	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  3098	   331	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);
  3099	   332	    assert.equal(res.generation, 2);
  3100	   333	
  3101	   334	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  3102	   335	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'a fresh writable generation');
  3103	   336	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reason, null, 'no carried reason');
  3104	   337	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'no carried class (would launder as high-confidence next hop)');
  3105	   338	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.finalizedAt, null, 'no carried finalizedAt (would poison the staleness reference)');
  3106	   339	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.toPlatformHint, null, 'no carried toPlatformHint');
  3107	   340	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(bundle.handoff.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.length >= 1, 'the receive_log audit chain is kept');
  3108	   341	  });
  3109	   342	
  3110	   343	  it('(iter-4 I3) a receive off the adopted generation does NOT auto-avoid the healthy origin', () => {
  3111	   344	    // End-to-end of the laundering fix: after a sealed usage-limit receive to
  3112	   345	    // claude-code, a NEXT receive (open generation, no seal) must re-classify at
  3113	   346	    // true confidence, not trust a carried class → claude-code stays selectable.
  3114	   347	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3115	   348	    const o1 = opts(); // claude-code -> codex per the helper default
  3116	   349	    commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, o1, io).token, o1, io);
  3117	   350	    // Now generation 2 is open, owned by codex. Receive it onward with a benign
  3118	   351	    // (non-limit) reason; the carried class must not drive avoidance.
  3119	   352	    const o2 = opts({ platform: 'claude-code', origin: 'codex', reason: 'switching back', sessionHint: 'cc-2' });
  3120	   353	    const { assignments } = prepare(ROOT, o2, io);
  3121	   354	    const avoidedCodex = Object.values(assignments).every((/** @type {any} */ a) =>
  3122	   355	      (a.skipped ?? []).some((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === 'codex' && s.why === 'avoided'),
  3123	   356	    );
  3124	   357	    assert.equal(avoidedCodex, false, 'no carried class silently avoids the adopted origin');
  3125	   358	  });
  3126	   359	});
  3127	   360	
  3128	   361	// ===========================================================================
  3129	   362	describe('receive-txn — chained failover A -> B -> C (checkpoints fired after each receive, F8)', () => {
  3130	   363	  // Plan §Concurrency: "chained A→B→C failover is contract-tested with checkpoints
  3131	   364	  // fired immediately after each receive". At this altitude a checkpoint is a
  3132	   365	  // journal append from the ADOPTED session; first-party = the event's sessionHint
  3133	   366	  // matches the live bundle's adopted origin.sessionHint and the event folds in.
  3134	   367	  it('receives twice with a first-party checkpoint after each; generations 1->2->3, ownership claude-code->codex->cursor', () => {
  3135	   368	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3136	   369	
  3137	   370	    // A (claude-code) -> B (codex).
  3138	   371	    const oAB = opts({ platform: 'codex', origin: 'claude-code', reason: 'A hit limit', sessionHint: 'codex-s' });
  3139	   372	    const rAB = commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, oAB, io).token, oAB, io);
  3140	   373	    assert.equal(rAB.generation, 2);
  3141	   374	
  3142	   375	    // Checkpoint immediately after the receive, from the adopted session.
  3143	   376	    appendJournal(ROOT, { type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'ck-B', source: 'stop', ts: NOW, sessionHint: 'codex-s', payload: { summary: 'B-first-party' } }, io);
  3144	   377	    const afterB = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
  3145	   378	    assert.equal(afterB.origin.platform, 'codex');
  3146	   379	    assert.equal(afterB.origin.sessionHint, 'codex-s', 'the checkpointing session IS the adopted owner — first-party by the isolation rule');
  3147	   380	    assert.ok(afterB.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'B-first-party'), "the adopted session's checkpoint folds into the new generation");
  3148	   381	
  3149	   382	    // B (codex, now the open owner) -> C (cursor). Prepared AFTER the checkpoint,
  3150	   383	    // so the fresh token binds the moved journalSeq — the intervening first-party
  3151	   384	    // checkpoint does NOT block a subsequent receive.
  3152	   385	    const oBC = opts({ platform: 'cursor', origin: 'codex', reason: "You've hit your usage limit", sessionHint: 'cursor-s' });
  3153	   386	    const rBC = commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, oBC, io).token, oBC, io);
  3154	   387	    assert.equal(rBC.generation, 3, 'the second receive opens generation 3');
  3155	   388	
  3156	   389	    // Checkpoint immediately after the second receive, from the newly adopted session.
  3157	   390	    appendJournal(ROOT, { type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'ck-C', source: 'stop', ts: NOW, sessionHint: 'cursor-s', payload: { summary: 'C-first-party' } }, io);
  3158	   391	    const afterC = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
  3159	   392	    assert.equal(afterC.generation, 3);
  3160	   393	    assert.equal(afterC.origin.platform, 'cursor', 'ownership walked to the final receiver');
  3161	   394	    assert.equal(afterC.origin.sessionHint, 'cursor-s');
  3162	   395	    assert.ok(afterC.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'C-first-party'), "the second receiver's checkpoint is first-party too");
  3163	   396	  });
  3164	   397	});

  3167	   400	describe('receive-txn.commit — token-binding rejections (one per bound input)', () => {
  3168	   401	  // Each: prepare a token, introduce ONE drift, then commit must throw naming
  3169	   402	  // re-prepare and leave the .handoff tree byte-identical to its pre-commit state.
  3170	   403	  const expectRejectedNoMutation = (io, doCommit) => {
  3171	   404	    const before = io.files();
  3172	   405	    assert.throws(doCommit, /re-?prepare/i, 'a bound-input drift must be rejected with an instruction to re-prepare');
  3173	   406	    assert.deepEqual(io.files(), before, 'a rejected commit must mutate nothing');
  3174	   407	  };

  3229	exec
  3230	/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba tests/unit/store.test.mjs | sed -n '1,280p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
  3231	 succeeded in 0ms:
  3232	     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
  3233	     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
  3234	     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
  3235	     4	import {
  3236	     5	  bundlePaths,
  3237	     6	  loadBundle,
  3238	     7	  writeSnapshot,
  3239	     8	  appendJournal,
  3240	     9	  rotateJournal,
  3241	    10	} from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
  3242	    11	import { applyEvent } from '../../core/src/bundle/merge.mjs';
  3243	    12	import { readAllTolerant } from '../../core/src/util/jsonl.mjs';
  3244	    13	
  3245	    14	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3246	    15	// Contract choices for bundle/store.mjs (docs/design/core.md §Module APIs,
  3247	    16	// §store test list; plan §Bundle, §Concurrency, §"Journal rotation & crash
  3248	    17	// recovery"). Readings pinned where the design left room:
  3249	    18	//
  3250	    19	//   1. PATHS — everything is rooted at `<root>/.handoff/`. `.bak` is the
  3251	    20	//      snapshot path + ".bak" (i.e. bundle.json.bak), matching fsx.backupThenWrite
  3252	    21	//      and fsx documentation. Exact set asserted in the bundlePaths test.
  3253	    22	//   2. loadBundle NULL — returns {bundle:null, warnings:[]} when the .handoff
  3254	    23	//      dir is absent OR neither a snapshot nor a journal exists. Warnings is
  3255	    24	//      ALWAYS an array of human-readable STRINGS (the API declares string[]);
  3256	    25	//      jsonl's structured {kind,line} warnings are surfaced as formatted strings,
  3257	    26	//      not raw objects.
  3258	    27	//   3. REPLAY — with a valid snapshot present, loadBundle applies exactly the
  3259	    28	//      journal entries whose seq > bundle.journalSeq, in journal (file) order,
  3260	    29	//      via merge.applyEvent. This must equal a pure left-fold of those same
  3261	    30	//      entries over the snapshot — the load path adds no hidden behavior. The
  3262	    31	//      seq>journalSeq FILTER is load-owned and independent of dedupe: the
  3263	    32	//      replay-boundary test drives a snapshot with an EMPTY dedupeRing so an
  3264	    33	//      implementation that wrongly folds the WHOLE journal cannot hide behind the
  3265	    34	//      ring — the pre-boundary events would visibly reappear in decisions.
  3266	    35	//   4. RECOVERY LADDER — snapshot that fails to parse OR fails validateBundle →
  3267	    36	//      try bundle.json.bak (parse+validate); if that is good, replay over IT
  3268	    37	//      (seq > bak.journalSeq). If BOTH snapshot and .bak are unusable → full
  3269	    38	//      rebuild by seeding an empty bundle and replaying the WHOLE journal. Every
  3270	    39	//      recovery emits at least one warning string.
  3271	    40	//   5. TORN TAIL — a torn final journal line is tolerated (jsonl drops it); the
  3272	    41	//      torn-tail warning is surfaced. Leftover *.tmp.* siblings are ignored by
  3273	    42	//      load (safeReadJson reads the real file).
  3274	    43	//   6. ROTATION FILE FORMAT — the history stem is
  3275	    44	//         `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`  where  tsForFile(iso)=iso.replace(/[:.]/g,'-')
  3276	    45	//      producing `history/<stem>.json` (frozen snapshot copy) and
  3277	    46	//      `history/<stem>.ndjson` (rotated journal). rotateJournal RETURNS the stem.
  3278	    47	//      The transform is pinned HERE so the manually-constructed crash states in
  3279	    48	//      the roll-forward/back tests name files exactly where loadBundle looks.
  3280	    49	//   7. ROTATION ORDER (crash-safe, plan §rotation): write
  3281	    50	//      `.handoff/rotation.marker.json` {kind, startedAt, seq} → freeze snapshot
  3282	    51	//      to history/<stem>.json → rename journal to history/<stem>.ndjson → start a
  3283	    52	//      fresh empty journal → clear the marker.
  3284	    53	//   8. MARKER RECONCILIATION — on load, a leftover marker rolls FORWARD when the
  3285	    54	//      freeze (history/<stem>.json, stem recomputed from marker.startedAt+kind)
  3286	    55	//      landed, else rolls BACK (clear marker, leave the live journal untouched).
  3287	    56	//      Both cases warn. Roll-forward completes from WHICHEVER step the crash
  3288	    57	//      interrupted: if the live journal has not yet been renamed to
  3289	    58	//      history/<stem>.ndjson, load performs that rename (losing no entries) and
  3290	    59	//      then seeds a fresh empty live journal; the near-complete state (freeze +
  3291	    60	//      rotated journal + empty live journal already present) is handled too.
  3292	    61	//   9. RETENTION — after a rotation, only the newest 10 rotations PER KIND are
  3293	    62	//      kept in history/ (both the .json and .ndjson of pruned rotations removed).
  3294	    63	//  10. writeSnapshot backs up the previous good snapshot to .bak, writes the new
  3295	    64	//      snapshot atomically (tmp+rename, via fsx), and renders HANDOFF.md
  3296	    65	//      alongside by delegating to render.mjs. This file proves the delegation
  3297	    66	//      via render's signature (the pinned footer + the goal) WITHOUT importing
  3298	    67	//      render — render.test owns the exact format.
  3299	    68	//  11. appendJournal runs UNDER THE REPO LOCK (see 12), ensures `.handoff/`
  3300	    69	//      exists, appends the entry via jsonl, and returns the entry's seq. SEQ
  3301	    70	//      ALLOCATION: an entry that already carries a numeric `seq` keeps it
  3302	    71	//      (adapter-normalized events arrive pre-numbered). An entry that LACKS a seq
  3303	    72	//      is assigned the next one under the lock — max(snapshot.journalSeq, highest
  3304	    73	//      seq already in the journal) + 1, monotonic across calls — and that
  3305	    74	//      allocated seq is both stamped onto the appended entry AND returned.
  3306	    75	//  12. REPO LOCK — writeSnapshot, appendJournal, and rotateJournal are MUTATIONS
  3307	    76	//      and run under bundle/lock.mjs's withLock (acquire → mutate → release).
  3308	    77	//      With a LIVE same-host foreign owner already holding `.handoff/lock/`, each
  3309	    78	//      throws LockHeldError (surfaced verbatim from the lock layer) and leaves
  3310	    79	//      every file byte-for-byte unchanged — a refused mutation is a no-op.
  3311	    80	//      loadBundle is a READ/recovery path and is NOT lock-gated (marker
  3312	    81	//      reconciliation is part of load).
  3313	    82	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3314	    83	
  3315	    84	const T0 = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
  3316	    85	const T1 = '2026-07-11T01:00:00.000Z';
  3317	    86	
  3318	    87	// A LIVE same-host foreign owner already holding the repo lock. processAlive
  3319	    88	// must report this pid alive so the lock layer refuses (never steals) it.
  3320	    89	const LOCK_OWNER = '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json';
  3321	    90	const liveForeignOwner = {
  3322	    91	  host: 'host-A', // same host as the default makeIo() identity
  3323	    92	  pid: 777, // a DIFFERENT process than this io's pid 4242
  3324	    93	  startTime: 42,
  3325	    94	  fencingToken: 'LIVE',
  3326	    95	  acquiredAt: T0,
  3327	    96	  heartbeatAt: T0,
  3328	    97	};

  3330	    99	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');

  3332	   101	function mkBundle(overrides = {}) {
  3333	   102	  const b = {
  3334	   103	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
  3335	   104	    bundleId: 'b_store000000000',
  3336	   105	    generation: 1,
  3337	   106	    createdAt: T0,
  3338	   107	    updatedAt: T0,
  3339	   108	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: null, unstable: false },
  3340	   109	    task: { goal: 'Build the store', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
  3341	   110	    plan: { steps: [] },
  3342	   111	    decisions: [],
  3343	   112	    files: { touched: [] },
  3344	   113	    roles: { assignments: {} },
  3345	   114	    git: null,
  3346	   115	    handoff: {
  3347	   116	      status: 'open',
  3348	   117	      reason: null,
  3349	   118	      reasonClass: null,
  3350	   119	      toPlatformHint: null,
  3351	   120	      finalizedAt: null,
  3352	   121	      receive_log: [],
  3353	   122	    },
  3354	   123	    journalSeq: 0,
  3355	   124	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
  3356	   125	    dedupeRing: [],
  3357	   126	  };
  3358	   127	  return { ...b, ...overrides };
  3359	   128	}
  3360	   129	
  3361	   130	function ev(overrides = {}) {
  3362	   131	  return {
  3363	   132	    seq: 1,
  3364	   133	    ts: T1,
  3365	   134	    type: 'note',
  3366	   135	    dedupeKey: 'k',
  3367	   136	    writerId: 'claude-code-1',
  3368	   137	    source: 'stop',
  3369	   138	    payload: {},
  3370	   139	    ...overrides,
  3371	   140	  };
  3372	   141	}
  3373	   142	
  3374	   143	// A seq-less event as an adapter that did NOT pre-number would hand it in — the
  3375	   144	// store must allocate the seq. `delete` (not `seq: undefined`) so the property is
  3376	   145	// truly absent, matching the "lacks a seq" branch.
  3377	   146	function evNoSeq(overrides = {}) {
  3378	   147	  const e = ev(overrides);
  3379	   148	  delete e.seq;
  3380	   149	  return e;
  3381	   150	}

  3383	   152	const snapText = (bundle) => JSON.stringify(bundle, null, 2) + '\n';
  3384	   153	const journalText = (entries) => (entries.length ? entries.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\n') + '\n' : '');

  3426	   195	
  3427	   196	  it('snapshot + journal replay EQUALS a pure reduction over the same events (seq > journalSeq)', () => {
  3428	   197	    const base = mkBundle();
  3429	   198	    const e1 = ev({ seq: 1, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'k1', payload: { summary: 'one' } });
  3430	   199	    const e2 = ev({ seq: 2, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'k2', payload: { summary: 'two' } });
  3431	   200	    const e3 = ev({ seq: 3, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'k3', payload: { summary: 'three' } });
  3432	   201	    const e4 = ev({ seq: 4, type: 'file.touch', dedupeKey: 'k4', payload: { path: 'a.mjs', op: 'edit' } });
  3433	   202	    const all = [e1, e2, e3, e4];
  3434	   203	
  3435	   204	    // The snapshot genuinely reflects e1,e2 (journalSeq becomes 2).
  3436	   205	    const snap = [e1, e2].reduce(applyEvent, base);
  3437	   206	    const io = makeIo({
  3438	   207	      files: {
  3439	   208	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap),
  3440	   209	        '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': journalText(all),
  3441	   210	      },
  3442	   211	    });
  3443	   212	
  3444	   213	    const { bundle, warnings } = loadBundle('/repo', io);
  3445	   214	    const expected = all.filter((e) => e.seq > snap.journalSeq).reduce(applyEvent, snap);
  3446	   215	    assert.deepEqual(bundle, expected, 'load must equal the pure fold of seq>journalSeq events over the snapshot');
  3447	   216	    assert.equal(bundle.journalSeq, 4);
  3448	   217	    assert.deepEqual(warnings, []);
  3449	   218	  });
  3450	   219	
  3451	   220	  it('replays ONLY entries with seq > journalSeq, proven with an EMPTY dedupeRing (no dedupe masking)', () => {
  3452	   221	    // The snapshot claims journalSeq 5 but carries an EMPTY dedupeRing. If the
  3453	   222	    // implementation folds the WHOLE journal instead of filtering on seq, the
  3454	   223	    // stale seq-3/4 events WOULD apply (nothing in the ring to no-op them) and
  3455	   224	    // their summaries would surface. Correct behavior applies only seq 6 and 7.
  3456	   225	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 5, dedupeRing: [] });
  3457	   226	    const e3 = ev({ seq: 3, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'stale3', payload: { summary: 'STALE-three' } });
  3458	   227	    const e4 = ev({ seq: 4, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'stale4', payload: { summary: 'STALE-four' } });
  3459	   228	    const e6 = ev({ seq: 6, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'new6', payload: { summary: 'NEW-six' } });
  3460	   229	    const e7 = ev({ seq: 7, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'new7', payload: { summary: 'NEW-seven' } });
  3461	   230	    const io = makeIo({
  3462	   231	      files: {
  3463	   232	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap),
  3464	   233	        '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': journalText([e3, e4, e6, e7]),
  3465	   234	      },
  3466	   235	    });
  3467	   236	
  3468	   237	    const { bundle, warnings } = loadBundle('/repo', io);
  3469	   238	    const summaries = bundle.decisions.map((d) => d.summary);
  3470	   239	    assert.ok(!summaries.includes('STALE-three'), 'seq 3 (<= journalSeq) must NOT be replayed');
  3471	   240	    assert.ok(!summaries.includes('STALE-four'), 'seq 4 (<= journalSeq) must NOT be replayed');
  3472	   241	    assert.ok(summaries.includes('NEW-six'), 'seq 6 (> journalSeq) must be applied');
  3473	   242	    assert.ok(summaries.includes('NEW-seven'), 'seq 7 (> journalSeq) must be applied');
  3474	   243	    assert.deepEqual(summaries, ['NEW-six', 'NEW-seven'], 'exactly and only the post-boundary events, in journal order');
  3475	   244	    assert.deepEqual(bundle, [e6, e7].reduce(applyEvent, snap), 'equals the pure fold of only seq>5 events');
  3476	   245	    assert.equal(bundle.journalSeq, 7);
  3477	   246	    assert.deepEqual(warnings, []);
  3478	   247	  });
  3479	   248	
  3480	   249	  it('ignores a leftover *.tmp.* sibling of the snapshot', () => {
  3481	   250	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 1 });
  3482	   251	    const io = makeIo({
  3483	   252	      files: {
  3484	   253	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap),
  3485	   254	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json.tmp.9': 'half-written-garbage{',
  3486	   255	      },

  3570	    14	/**
  3571	    15	 * Rename with retry on EPERM (Windows AV/indexer lock pattern): 3 attempts total.
  3572	    16	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} from @param {string} to
  3573	    17	 */
  3574	    18	function renameWithRetry(fs, from, to) {
  3575	    19	  let lastErr;
  3576	    20	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
  3577	    21	    try {
  3578	    22	      fs.renameSync(from, to);
  3579	    23	      return;
  3580	    24	    } catch (err) {
  3581	    25	      lastErr = err;
  3582	    26	      if (!err || /** @type {any} */ (err).code !== 'EPERM') throw err;
  3583	    27	    }
  3584	    28	  }
  3585	    29	  throw lastErr;
  3586	    30	}

  3851	   563	    assert.equal(after[`${paths.historyDir}/${stem}.json`], snapText(snap), 'frozen snapshot copy');
  3852	   564	    assert.equal(after[`${paths.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`], journalText(entries), 'rotated journal copy');
  3853	   565	
  3854	   566	    // live journal is now empty; marker gone.
  3855	   567	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(paths.journal), 'a fresh live journal exists');
  3856	   568	    assert.equal(readAllTolerant(io.fs, paths.journal).entries.length, 0, 'the live journal was emptied');
  3857	   569	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(paths.dir + '/rotation.marker.json'), false, 'the marker is cleared on success');
  3858	   570	  });
  3859	   571	
  3860	   572	  it('retention: keeps only the newest 10 rotations PER KIND', () => {
  3861	   573	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 0 });
  3862	   574	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap) } });
  3863	   575	    const paths = bundlePaths('/repo');
  3864	   576	
  3865	   577	    // Two takeover rotations — these must survive finalize pruning (per-kind).
  3866	   578	    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
  3867	   579	      io.setNow(`2026-07-11T10:0${i}:00.000Z`);
  3868	   580	      appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 200 + i, dedupeKey: `tk${i}` }), io);
  3869	   581	      rotateJournal('/repo', 'takeover', io);
  3870	   582	    }
  3871	   583	
  3872	   584	    // Eleven finalize rotations — the oldest must be pruned to 10.
  3873	   585	    const finalizeStems = [];
  3874	   586	    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i += 1) {
  3875	   587	      const mm = String(i).padStart(2, '0');
  3876	   588	      io.setNow(`2026-07-11T11:${mm}:00.000Z`);
  3877	   589	      appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 1 + i, dedupeKey: `fk${i}` }), io);
  3878	   590	      finalizeStems.push(rotateJournal('/repo', 'finalize', io));
  3879	   591	    }

  3883	   595	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.finalize\.ndjson$/.test(n)).length, 10, 'exactly 10 finalize journals retained');
  3884	   596	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.takeover\.json$/.test(n)).length, 2, 'takeover rotations pruned independently');
  3885	   597	
  3886	   598	    assert.ok(!names.includes(`${finalizeStems[0]}.json`), 'the oldest finalize freeze was pruned');
  3887	   599	    assert.ok(names.includes(`${finalizeStems[10]}.json`), 'the newest finalize freeze was kept');
  3888	   600	  });
  3889	   601	});
  3890	   602	
  3891	   603	describe('store — mutations run under the repo lock', () => {
  3892	   604	  // With a LIVE same-host foreign owner holding `.handoff/lock/`, every store
  3893	   605	  // MUTATION must be refused by the lock layer (LockHeldError) and leave the
  3894	   606	  // whole `.handoff/` tree byte-for-byte unchanged. This is what proves the
  3895	   607	  // mutations actually acquire the lock rather than writing unlocked.
  3896	   608	  const withLiveForeignLock = (files) =>
  3897	   609	    makeIo({
  3898	   610	      files: { [LOCK_OWNER]: JSON.stringify(liveForeignOwner), ...files },
  3899	   611	      processAlive: (pid) => pid === 777 || pid === 4242, // pid 777 (the owner) is alive
  3900	   612	    });
  3901	   613	  const isLockHeld = (e) => e && e.name === 'LockHeldError';
  3902	   614	
  3903	   615	  it('writeSnapshot throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  3904	   616	    const prev = mkBundle({ task: { goal: 'PREV goal', constraints: [], acceptance: [] } });
  3905	   617	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({ '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(prev) });
  3906	   618	    const before = io.files();
  3907	   619	
  3908	   620	    assert.throws(
  3909	   621	      () => writeSnapshot('/repo', mkBundle({ generation: 2, task: { goal: 'NEW goal', constraints: [], acceptance: [] } }), io),
  3910	   622	      isLockHeld,
  3911	   623	    );
  3912	   624	
  3913	   625	    const after = io.files();
  3914	   626	    assert.deepEqual(after, before, 'no file may change when a live foreign owner holds the lock');
  3915	   627	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json'], snapText(prev), 'the existing snapshot is untouched');
  3916	   628	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json.bak'], undefined, 'no .bak is written under a refused lock');
  3917	   629	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/HANDOFF.md'], undefined, 'HANDOFF.md is not rendered under a refused lock');
  3918	   630	    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(after[LOCK_OWNER]), liveForeignOwner, 'the foreign owner.json is never modified');
  3919	   631	  });
  3920	   632	
  3921	   633	  it('appendJournal throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  3922	   634	    const existing = [ev({ seq: 6, dedupeKey: 'e6' })];
  3923	   635	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({ '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': journalText(existing) });
  3924	   636	    const before = io.files();
  3925	   637	
  3926	   638	    assert.throws(() => appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 7, dedupeKey: 'e7' }), io), isLockHeld);
  3927	   639	    assert.throws(() => appendJournal('/repo', evNoSeq({ dedupeKey: 'e8' }), io), isLockHeld);
  3928	   640	
  3929	   641	    assert.deepEqual(io.files(), before, 'the journal is not appended (and no seq allocated) under a refused lock');
  3930	   642	    assert.equal(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson'], journalText(existing), 'the journal bytes are unchanged');
  3931	   643	  });
  3932	   644	
  3933	   645	  it('rotateJournal throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  3934	   646	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 5 });
  3935	   647	    const entries = [ev({ seq: 4, dedupeKey: 'a' }), ev({ seq: 5, dedupeKey: 'b' })];
  3936	   648	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({

  3943	   655

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba tests/helpers/fakeio.mjs | sed -n '1,220p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
    92	  };
    93	}

   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
   140	
   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
   142	
   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
   159	
   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the

 succeeded in 0ms:
    69	### `baton loop` (Layer 2)
    70	
    71	- `baton loop init <goal>` — scaffold spec + plan template; idempotent,
    72	  dry-run-able, same two-phase plan/apply discipline as `baton init`.
    73	- `baton loop run [--approve-smoke <token>]` — the state machine. State lives
    74	  in gitignored `.handoff/loop/state.json` + `journal.ndjson` (same atomic
    75	  write + journal replay machinery as the bundle; loop position is also
    76	  checkpointed into the handoff bundle so a limit death carries it).
    77	
    78	### Root + ownership discipline (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 2)
    79	
    80	One authoritative root: the MAIN repo root where `loop.json` lives. The
    81	supervisor is the SOLE writer of `.handoff/loop/` and of the main root's
    82	handoff bundle; every supervisor-driven baton invocation passes explicit
    83	`--root <mainRoot>` and a supervisor-owned stable session hint
    84	(`--session loop-<runId>` — a NEW flag added to `checkpoint`; `receive`
    85	already has it), so the bundle has exactly one stable owner.
    86	
    87	**Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3,
    88	finding 1)** — children never touch OR read any bundle, by construction: the
    89	supervisor sets `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1` in every child's environment, and
    90	EVERY baton hook command — the hook entrypoints, `baton checkpoint`, and
    91	`baton session-start` (which would otherwise read the bundle and inject
    92	pending-handoff resume context into the child) — quietly no-ops (exit 0,
    93	empty output) when it is set. This covers
    94	Layer-2 children running in the MAIN root (where root discovery would
    95	otherwise find the supervisor-owned bundle and the foreign-session guard
    96	would reject their stable hints) and pipeline children in worktrees alike;

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/loop/spec.mjs | sed -n '1,220p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop spec (baton/loop@1) — pure load/validate for the Layer-2 goal loop.
     3	 * The spec is committed project config (loop.json at the repo root); run
     4	 * state lives elsewhere (.handoff/loop/). Plan:
     5	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Loop spec".
     6	 */
     7	
     8	export const LOOP_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop@1';
     9	
    10	// The 5-cap is a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md §Review gates): a spec
    11	// asking for a 6th gate iteration is invalid, not a preference.
    12	export const ITERATION_CAP_MAX = 5;
    13	
    14	const DEFAULT_BUDGETS = Object.freeze({ iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 });
    15	
    16	/**
    17	 * The scaffold `baton loop init` writes. Phase ids are progress labels;
    18	 * every role is a matrix role ID resolved via baton.config.json — never a
    19	 * model name.
    20	 * @param {string} goal
    21	 * @returns {any}
    22	 */
    23	export function defaultLoopSpec(goal) {
    24	  return {
    25	    schema: LOOP_SCHEMA,
    26	    goal,
    27	    constraints: [],
    28	    smoke: { cmd: null, expect: null },
    29	    phases: [
    30	      { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' },
    31	      { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' },
    32	      // The smoke slice is itself a TDD mini-cycle (Gate-1 iter-2 finding 2):
    33	      // failing smoke assertion → verifier approval → minimal implementation.
    34	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
    35	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
    36	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
    37	      { id: 'subtask-tests', role: 'test-author' },
    38	      { id: 'subtask-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
    39	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
    40	      { id: 'subtask-review', role: 'subtask-reviewer' },
    41	      { id: 'gate-2-a', role: 'final-reviewer-a' },
    42	      { id: 'gate-2-b', role: 'final-reviewer-b' },
    43	    ],
    44	    budgets: { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS },
    45	  };
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * Validate a loop spec against the role matrix. Reports EVERY problem at
    50	 * once (each error names its offender) so a spec author fixes one round, not
    51	 * one field per run. Valid specs are returned normalized: caller-provided
    52	 * fields are preserved verbatim; only ABSENT constraints/budgets/smoke are
    53	 * filled with defaults.
    54	 * @param {any} spec
    55	 * @param {any} config the parsed baton.config.json (roles are checked against it)
    56	 * @returns {{ok: true, spec: any} | {ok: false, errors: string[]}}
    57	 */
    58	export function validateLoopSpec(spec, config) {
    59	  /** @type {string[]} */
    60	  const errors = [];
    61	  const s = spec ?? {};
    62	
    63	  if (s.schema !== LOOP_SCHEMA) errors.push(`schema must be '${LOOP_SCHEMA}' (got ${JSON.stringify(s.schema)})`);
    64	  if (typeof s.goal !== 'string' || s.goal.length === 0) errors.push('goal must be a non-empty string');
    65	
    66	  let constraints = s.constraints;
    67	  if (constraints === undefined) constraints = [];
    68	  if (!Array.isArray(constraints)) {
    69	    errors.push('constraints must be an array of strings');
    70	  } else {
    71	    constraints.forEach((c, i) => {
    72	      if (typeof c !== 'string') errors.push(`constraints[${i}] must be a string (got ${JSON.stringify(c)})`);
    73	    });
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  const roles = config?.roles ?? {};
    77	  if (!Array.isArray(s.phases) || s.phases.length === 0) {
    78	    errors.push('phases must be a non-empty array of {id, role}');
    79	  } else {
    80	    s.phases.forEach((/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    81	      if (typeof p?.id !== 'string' || p.id.length === 0) errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing an id (a non-empty progress label)`);
    82	      if (typeof p?.role !== 'string' || p.role.length === 0) {
    83	        errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing a role (a baton.config.json role ID)`);
    84	      } else if (!(p.role in roles)) {
    85	        errors.push(`phases[${i}]: unknown role '${p.role}' — add it to baton.config.json roles or fix the reference`);
    86	      }
    87	    });
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  let budgets = s.budgets;
    91	  if (budgets === undefined) {
    92	    budgets = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS };
    93	  } else if (typeof budgets !== 'object' || budgets === null) {
    94	    errors.push('budgets must be an object');
    95	  } else {
    96	    const cap = budgets.iterationCap;
    97	    if (typeof cap !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(cap) || cap < 1 || cap > ITERATION_CAP_MAX) {
    98	      errors.push(`budgets.iterationCap must be a number between 1 and ${ITERATION_CAP_MAX} (the 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)})`);
    99	    }
   100	    for (const field of ['perRoleTimeoutMin', 'maxChildrenPerPhase']) {
   101	      const v = budgets[field];
   102	      if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) {
   103	        errors.push(`budgets.${field} must be a positive number (got ${JSON.stringify(v)})`);
   104	      }
   105	    }
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  let smoke = s.smoke;
   109	  if (smoke === undefined) {
   110	    smoke = { cmd: null, expect: null };
   111	  } else if (typeof smoke !== 'object' || smoke === null) {
   112	    errors.push('smoke must be an object ({cmd, expect}, each a string or null)');
   113	  } else {
   114	    if (!(smoke.cmd === null || typeof smoke.cmd === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.cmd must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.cmd)})`);
   115	    if (!(smoke.expect === null || typeof smoke.expect === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.expect must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.expect)})`);
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
   119	  return { ok: true, spec: { ...s, constraints, budgets, smoke } };
   120	}

    63	// A config carrying every role the default spec references (the real matrix ids).
    64	function fullConfig() {
    65	  return {
